    44: def _utc_now_iso() -> str:
    45:     """Timezone-aware UTC timestamp, formatted with a trailing 'Z'.
    46: 
    47:     Replaces deprecated datetime.utcnow() (removed-path in Python 3.12+).
    48:     """
    49:     return datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat().replace("+00:00", "Z")
    50: 
    51: 
    52: def _utc_from_timestamp_iso(ts: float) -> str:
    53:     return datetime.fromtimestamp(ts, tz=timezone.utc).isoformat().replace("+00:00", "Z")
    54:

    60: _TAG_STRIP_RE = re.compile(r"<[^>]+>")
    61: _WS_SAFE_RE = re.compile(r"[^a-z0-9_]+", re.IGNORECASE)
    62: 
    63: 
    64: def _strip_tags_basic(s: str) -> str:
    65:     s = _TAG_STRIP_RE.sub(" ", s or "")
    66:     s = re.sub(r"\s+", " ", s).strip()
    67:     return s

   665:     def _merge_unique(existing: List[str], new_values: List[str] | None) -> List[str]:
   666:         out = [str(x).strip() for x in existing if str(x).strip()]
   667:         seen = set(out)
   668:         for v in new_values or []:
   669:             s = str(v).strip()
   670:             if s and s not in seen:
   671:                 out.append(s)
   672:                 seen.add(s)
   673:         return out

   675:     # Runtime document membership must REPLACE, not merge.
   676:     # This prevents phrases from multiple uploaded documents being active together.
   677:     if add_document_ids is not None:
   678:         obj["active_document_ids"] = [
   679:             str(x).strip()

   682:         ]

   719: @router.post("/upload")
   720: async def upload_file(
   721:     workspace_id: str = Query("ws_betterhealthcheck_com"),
   722:     file: UploadFile = File(...),
   723: ):
   724:     if not file or not file.filename:
   725:         raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="No file uploaded.")
   726: 
   727:     ext = _guess_ext(file.filename)
   728:     if ext not in ALLOWED_EXT:
   729:         raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail=f"File type not allowed: {ext}")

    84:     if not ws:
    85:         raise ValueError("missing_workspace_id")
    86:     if not ws.startswith("ws_"):
    87:         raise ValueError("workspace_id_must_start_with_ws_")

   100: def _active_target_set_path(ws: str) -> Path:
   101:     return Path("backend/server/data/target_pools") / f"active_target_set_{ws}.json"
   102: 
   103: 
   104: def _now_ts() -> float:
   105:     return time.time()
   106: 
   107: 
   108: def _time_left(deadline_ts: float | None) -> float:
   109:     if deadline_ts is None:
   110:         return 999999.0
   111:     return max(0.0, float(deadline_ts) - _now_ts())
   112: 
   113: 
   114: def _near_deadline(deadline_ts: float | None, *, min_left: float = 1.5) -> bool:
   115:     return _time_left(deadline_ts) <= float(min_left)
   116: 
   117: 
   118: _CHARSET_RE = re.compile(r"charset\s*=\s*([a-zA-Z0-9_\-:]+)", re.IGNORECASE)
   119: 
   120: 
   121: def _decode_html_bytes(content: bytes, content_type: str = "") -> str:
   122:     """
   123:     Decode bounded HTML bytes to text, honoring charset from Content-Type when present.
   124:     Falls back safely to avoid mojibake later.
   125:     """
   126:     if not isinstance(content, (bytes, bytearray)):
   127:         return ""
   128: 
   129:     if len(content) > MAX_HTML_BYTES:
   130:         content = content[:MAX_HTML_BYTES]
   131: 
   132:     ct = (content_type or "").lower()
   133:     enc = None
   134:     m = _CHARSET_RE.search(ct)
   135:     if m:
   136:         enc = (m.group(1) or "").strip().strip('"').strip("'")
   137: 
   138:     if enc:
   139:         try:
   140:             return content.decode(enc, errors="strict")
   141:         except Exception:
   142:             pass
   143: 
   144:     for fallback in ("utf-8", "windows-1252", "latin-1"):
   145:         try:
   146:             return content.decode(fallback, errors="strict" if fallback == "utf-8" else "replace")
   147:         except Exception:
   148:             continue
   149: 
   150:     return content.decode("latin-1", errors="replace")
   151: 
   152: 
   153: def _http_get(
   154:     url: str,
   155:     *,
   156:     deadline_ts: float | None = None,

    68: // --- LinkCraftor: XML sitemap helper (reads <loc> URLs) ---
    69: // NOTE: kept for compatibility / debugging, not required for backend import flow.
    70: function lcImportSitemapXML(file, onDone) {
    71:   const reader = new FileReader();
    72: 
    73:   reader.onload = (e) => {
    74:     const text = e.target.result;
    75: 
    76:     const parser = new DOMParser();
    77:     const xmlDoc = parser.parseFromString(text, "application/xml");
    78: 
    79:     const locNodes = xmlDoc.getElementsByTagName("loc");
    80:     const entries = [];
    81: 
    82:     for (let i = 0; i < locNodes.length; i++) {
    83:       const url = (locNodes[i].textContent || "").trim();
    84:       if (url) {
    85:         entries.push({ URL: url });
    86:       }
    87:     }
    88: 
    89:     console.log("? lcImportSitemapXML:", entries);
    90: 
    91:     if (typeof onDone === "function") {
    92:       onDone(entries);
    93:     }
    94:   };
    95: 
    96:   reader.readAsText(file);
    97: }

    72: // NOTE: kept for compatibility / debugging, not required for backend import flow.
    73: function lcImportSitemapXML(file, onDone) {
    74:   const reader = new FileReader();
    75: 
    76:   reader.onload = (e) => {
    77:     const text = e.target.result;
    78: 
    79:     const parser = new DOMParser();
    80:     const xmlDoc = parser.parseFromString(text, "application/xml");
    81: 
    82:     const locNodes = xmlDoc.getElementsByTagName("loc");
    83:     const entries = [];
    84: 
    85:     for (let i = 0; i < locNodes.length; i++) {
    86:       const url = (locNodes[i].textContent || "").trim();
    87:       if (url) {
    88:         entries.push({ URL: url });
    89:       }
    90:     }
    91: 
    92:     console.log("? lcImportSitemapXML:", entries);
    93: 
    94:     if (typeof onDone === "function") {
    95:       onDone(entries);
    96:     }
    97:   };
    98: 
    99:   reader.readAsText(file);
   100: }

  3915: 
  3916:   try {
  3917:     const res = await fetch(`${API_BASE}/sitemap/fetch-content`, {
  3918:       method: "POST",
  3919:       headers: {
  3920:         "Content-Type": "application/json"
  3921:       },
  3922:       body: JSON.stringify({ urls })
  3923:     });
  3924: 
  3925:     if (!res.ok) {
  3926:       const errText = await res.text().catch(() => "");

  3928:       showToast(errorBox, `Sitemap content fetch failed (${res.status}).`, 2000);
  3929:       return [];
  3930:     }
  3931: 
  3932:     const data = await res.json();
  3933:     // Expected format: { items: [ { url, title, content, status }, ... ] }
  3934:     if (DEBUG) console.log("[sitemap] fetched content:", data);
  3935: 
  3936:     return Array.isArray(data.items) ? data.items : [];
  3937:   } catch (err) {

  6278:   }

  6280:   // ? Normal replace when backend has data (or when we had nothing yet)
  6281:   set.clear();
  6282:   for (const u of urls) set.add(u);
  6283: 
  6284:   return set.size;
  6285: }

   258: def _runtime_normalized_score(source_type: str, intelligence: Dict[str, Any], target_score: Any) -> float:
   259:     try:
   260:         raw = float(target_score or 0)
   261:     except Exception:
   262:         raw = 0.0
   263: 
   264:     if source_type in {"draft", "document_registry"}:
   265:         return max(0.0, min(1.0, raw))
   266: 
   267:     # Live-domain/imported legacy scores commonly use larger numeric scales.
   268:     if source_type in {"live_domain", "imported"}:
   269:         return max(0.0, min(1.0, raw / 300.0))
   270: 
   271:     return max(0.0, min(1.0, raw))
   272: 
   273: 
   274: def _meaningful_tokens(text: str) -> set[str]:
   275:     stop = {
   276:         "a", "an", "and", "are", "as", "at", "be", "by", "for", "from",
   277:         "how", "in", "into", "is", "it", "of", "on", "or", "the", "to",
   278:         "what", "when", "where", "why", "with", "your", "you"
   279:     }
   280:     tokens = re.findall(r"[a-z0-9]+", str(text or "").lower())
   281:     return {_stem_token(t) for t in tokens if len(t) >= 3 and t not in stop}
   282:

   475: 
   476: def _load_active_target_set(workspace_id: str) -> Dict[str, Any]:
   477:     fp = _active_target_set_path(workspace_id)
   478:     if not fp.exists():
   479:         return {}
   480:     try:
   481:         obj = json.loads(fp.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
   482:         return obj if isinstance(obj, dict) else {}
   483:     except Exception:
   484:         return {}
   485: 
   486: 
   487: def _norm_url_for_active(value: Any) -> str:
   488:     return str(value or "").strip().rstrip("/").lower()
   489: 
   490: 
   491: def _active_values(active: Dict[str, Any], key: str) -> set[str]:
   492:     return {
   493:         _norm_url_for_active(x)
   494:         for x in (active.get(key) or [])
   495:         if str(x or "").strip()
   496:     }
   497: 
   498: 
   499: def _filter_targets_by_active_membership(
   500:     workspace_id: str,
   501:     source_type: str,
   502:     targets: List[Dict[str, Any]],
   503: ) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:
   504:     active = _load_active_target_set(workspace_id)
   505:     if not active:
   506:         return targets
   507: 
   508:     live_urls = _active_values(active, "active_live_domain_urls")
   509:     imported_urls = _active_values(active, "active_imported_urls")
   510:     draft_ids = _active_values(active, "active_draft_ids")
   511:     document_ids = _active_values(active, "active_document_ids")
   512: 
   513:     # If the active file exists but all memberships are empty, do not wipe the resolver.
   514:     if not (live_urls or imported_urls or draft_ids or document_ids):
   515:         return targets
   516: 
   517:     # If this source has no active membership list, it should not participate.
   518:     if source_type == "live_domain" and not live_urls:
   519:         return []
   520:     if source_type == "imported" and not imported_urls:
   521:         return []
   522:     if source_type == "draft" and not draft_ids:
   523:         return []
   524:     if source_type == "document_registry" and not document_ids:
   525:         return []
   526: 
   527:     out: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []

   573: def _enrich_resolver_results_v2(
   574:     *,
   575:     workspace_id: str,
   576:     anchor_phrase: str,
   577:     results: List[Dict[str, Any]],
   578:     limit: int = 5,
   579: ) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:
   580:     final_results = list(results or [])[: max(1, int(limit or 5))]
   581: 
   582:     if not final_results:
   583:         return []
   584: 
   585:     competition_intelligence = analyze_resolver_competition_v1(final_results)
   586: 
   587:     for item in final_results:
   588:         item["competition_intelligence"] = {
   589:             "has_competition_analysis": competition_intelligence.get("has_competition_analysis"),
   590:             "has_runner_up": competition_intelligence.get("has_runner_up"),
   591:             "winner_score": competition_intelligence.get("winner_score"),
   592:             "runner_up_score": competition_intelligence.get("runner_up_score"),
   593:             "score_margin": competition_intelligence.get("score_margin"),
   594:             "close_call": competition_intelligence.get("close_call"),
   595:             "competition_confidence": competition_intelligence.get("competition_confidence"),
   596:             "competition_action": competition_intelligence.get("competition_action"),
   597:             "competition_reason": competition_intelligence.get("competition_reason"),
   598:         }
   599: 
   600:         url = item.get("url") or item.get("target_url") or ""
   601: 
   602:         item.setdefault(
   603:             "topic_authority_intelligence",
   604:             analyze_topic_authority_v1(item),
   605:         )
   606: 
   607:         item.setdefault(
   608:             "entity_intelligence",
   609:             analyze_entity_intelligence_v1(anchor_phrase, item),
   610:         )
   611: 
   612:         item.setdefault(
   613:             "performance_intelligence",
   614:             analyze_performance_v1(workspace_id=workspace_id, target_url=url),
   615:         )
   616: 
   617:         item.setdefault(
   618:             "cross_site_intelligence",
   619:             analyze_cross_site_intelligence_v1(

   187: def build_document_registry(workspace_id: str) -> Dict[str, Any]:
   188:     inputs = load_cross_document_inputs(workspace_id)
   189:     ws = inputs["workspace_id"]
   190: 
   191:     docs = _extract_docs_from_struct(inputs["upload_struct"])
   192:     phrases_by_doc = _extract_phrases_by_doc(inputs["upload_phrase_pool"])
   193:     entities_by_doc = _extract_entities_by_doc(inputs["upload_entity_graph"])
   194: 
   195:     active_ids = inputs["active_phrase_set"].get("active_document_ids", [])
   196:     if not isinstance(active_ids, list):
   197:         active_ids = []
   198: 
   199:     registry: Dict[str, Dict[str, Any]] = {}
   200: 
   201:     all_doc_ids = set(docs.keys()) | set(phrases_by_doc.keys()) | set(entities_by_doc.keys())
   202: 
   203:     for doc_id in sorted(all_doc_ids):
   204:         doc_obj = docs.get(doc_id, {}) if isinstance(docs.get(doc_id), dict) else {}
   205: 
   206:         title = (
   207:             doc_obj.get("title")
   208:             or doc_obj.get("filename")
   209:             or doc_obj.get("name")
   210:             or doc_id
   211:         )
   212: 
   213:         registry[doc_id] = {
   214:             "document_id": doc_id,
   215:             "title": str(title),
   216:             "is_active": doc_id in active_ids,
   217:             "phrase_count": len(phrases_by_doc.get(doc_id, set())),
   218:             "entity_count": len(entities_by_doc.get(doc_id, set())),
   219:             "phrases": sorted(phrases_by_doc.get(doc_id, set())),
   220:             "entities": sorted(entities_by_doc.get(doc_id, set())),
   221:         }
   222: 
   223:     return {
   224:         "workspace_id": ws,
   225:         "type": "cross_document_registry",
   226:         "generated_at": _now_iso(),
   227:         "documents_count": len(registry),
   228:         "active_document_ids_count": len(active_ids),
   229:         "source_files_exist": inputs["exists"],
   230:         "documents": registry,
   231:         "runtime_effect": "read_only_no_runtime_injection",
   232:     }
   233: 
   234: def _jaccard_similarity(a: Set[str], b: Set[str]) -> float:
   235:     if not a or not b:
   236:         return 0.0
   237: 
   238:     union = a | b
   239:     if not union:
   240:         return 0.0
   241: 
   242:     intersection = a & b
   243:     return round(len(intersection) / len(union), 4)

     9: def _data_dir() -> Path:
    10:     here = Path(__file__).resolve()
    11:     server_dir = here.parents[2]
    12:     return server_dir / "data"
    13: 
    14: 
    15: def _ws_safe(ws: str) -> str:
    16:     raw = (ws or "default").strip()
    17:     if not raw:
    18:         return "default"
    19:     if raw.lower().startswith("ws_"):
    20:         return raw
    21:     s = raw.lower().replace(".", "_").replace("-", "_").replace(" ", "_")
    22:     s = re.sub(r"[^a-z0-9_]+", "_", s)
    23:     s = re.sub(r"_+", "_", s).strip("_")
    24:     return f"ws_{s or 'workspace'}"[:80]
    25: 
    26: 
    27: def _pool_path(workspace_id: str) -> Path:
    28:     ws = _ws_safe(workspace_id)
    29:     return (
    30:         _data_dir()
    31:         / "target_pools"
    32:         / "document_registry"
    33:         / f"document_registry_{ws}.json"
    34:     )
    35: 
    36: 
    37: def _safe_read_json(path: Path) -> Any:
    38:     try:
    39:         return json.loads(path.read_text(encoding="utf-8-sig"))
    40:     except Exception:
    41:         return None
    42: 
    43: 
    44: def _norm_text(value: Any) -> str:
    45:     s = str(value or "").lower()
    46:     s = re.sub(r"<[^>]+>", " ", s)
    47:     s = re.sub(r"[^a-z0-9]+", " ", s)
    48:     s = re.sub(r"\s+", " ", s).strip()
    49:     return s
    50: 
    51: 
    52: def _tokens(value: Any) -> List[str]:
    53:     return [t for t in _norm_text(value).split() if len(t) > 2]
    54: 
    55: 
    56: def _jaccard(a: List[str], b: List[str]) -> float:
    57:     sa, sb = set(a), set(b)
    58:     if not sa or not sb:
    59:         return 0.0
    60:     return len(sa & sb) / max(1, len(sa | sb))
    61: 
    62: 
    63: def _clamp(x: float, lo: float = 0.0, hi: float = 1.0) -> float:
    64:     return max(lo, min(hi, float(x)))
    65: 
    66: 
    67: def load_document_registry_targets(workspace_id: str) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:
    68:     obj = _safe_read_json(_pool_path(workspace_id))
    69:     if not isinstance(obj, dict):
    70:         return []
    71:     items = obj.get("items") or []
    72:     if not isinstance(items, list):
    73:         return []
    74:     return [x for x in items if isinstance(x, dict)]
    75: 
    76: 
    77: def semantic_route_score(phrase: str, target: Dict[str, Any]) -> float:
    78:     phrase_tokens = _tokens(phrase)
    79:     target_text = " ".join([
    80:         str(target.get("title") or ""),
    81:         str(target.get("label") or ""),
    82:         str(target.get("h1") or ""),
    83:     ])
    84:     target_tokens = _tokens(target_text)
    85: 
    86:     score = _jaccard(phrase_tokens, target_tokens)
    87: 
    88:     phrase_norm = _norm_text(phrase)

    23:     s = re.sub(r"_+", "_", s).strip("_")
    24:     return f"ws_{s or 'workspace'}"[:80]
    25: 
    26: 
    27: def _pool_path(workspace_id: str) -> Path:
    28:     ws = _ws_safe(workspace_id)
    29:     return (
    30:         _data_dir()
    31:         / "target_pools"
    32:         / "document_registry"
    33:         / f"document_registry_{ws}.json"
    34:     )
    35: 
    36: 
    37: def _safe_read_json(path: Path) -> Any:
    38:     try:
    39:         return json.loads(path.read_text(encoding="utf-8-sig"))
    40:     except Exception:
    41:         return None
    42: 
    43: 
    44: def _norm_text(value: Any) -> str:
    45:     s = str(value or "").lower()
    46:     s = re.sub(r"<[^>]+>", " ", s)
    47:     s = re.sub(r"[^a-z0-9]+", " ", s)
    48:     s = re.sub(r"\s+", " ", s).strip()
    49:     return s
    50: 
    51: 
    52: def _tokens(value: Any) -> List[str]:
    53:     return [t for t in _norm_text(value).split() if len(t) > 2]
    54: 
    55: 
    56: def _jaccard(a: List[str], b: List[str]) -> float:
    57:     sa, sb = set(a), set(b)
    58:     if not sa or not sb:
    59:         return 0.0
    60:     return len(sa & sb) / max(1, len(sa | sb))
    61: 
    62: 
    63: def _clamp(x: float, lo: float = 0.0, hi: float = 1.0) -> float:
    64:     return max(lo, min(hi, float(x)))
    65: 
    66: 
    67: def load_document_registry_targets(workspace_id: str) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:
    68:     obj = _safe_read_json(_pool_path(workspace_id))
    69:     if not isinstance(obj, dict):
    70:         return []
    71:     items = obj.get("items") or []
    72:     if not isinstance(items, list):
    73:         return []
    74:     return [x for x in items if isinstance(x, dict)]
    75: 
    76: 
    77: def semantic_route_score(phrase: str, target: Dict[str, Any]) -> float:
    78:     phrase_tokens = _tokens(phrase)

    83:     ])
    84:     target_tokens = _tokens(target_text)
    85: 
    86:     score = _jaccard(phrase_tokens, target_tokens)
    87: 
    88:     phrase_norm = _norm_text(phrase)
    89:     target_norm = _norm_text(target_text)

    77: def semantic_route_score(phrase: str, target: Dict[str, Any]) -> float:
    78:     phrase_tokens = _tokens(phrase)
    79:     target_text = " ".join([
    80:         str(target.get("title") or ""),
    81:         str(target.get("label") or ""),
    82:         str(target.get("h1") or ""),

    92:         score += 0.35
    93: 
    94:     if target_norm and target_norm in phrase_norm:
    95:         score += 0.15
    96: 
    97:     return _clamp(score)

    99: 
   100: def semantic_intent_score(phrase: str, target: Dict[str, Any]) -> float:
   101:     signals = target.get("semantic_intent_signals") or {}
   102:     phrase_norm = _norm_text(phrase)
   103: 
   104:     score = 0.35
   105: 
   106:     if signals.get("has_tool_intent") and any(x in phrase_norm for x in ["calculator", "tool", "checker", "estimator"]):
   107:         score += 0.25
   108: 
   109:     if signals.get("has_guide_intent") and any(x in phrase_norm for x in ["guide", "how", "what", "complete", "explained"]):
   110:         score += 0.25
   111: 
   112:     if signals.get("has_question_intent") and any(x in phrase_norm.split() for x in ["how", "what", "why", "when", "where"]):
   113:         score += 0.15
   114: 
   115:     return _clamp(score)
   116: 
   117: 
   118: def authority_score(target: Dict[str, Any]) -> float:
   119:     source = str(target.get("title_source") or "")
   120:     if source in {"h1", "first_heading"}:
   121:         return 0.82
   122:     if source == "filename":
   123:         return 0.62

   185: def semantic_gate_allows(phrase: str, target: Dict[str, Any], min_score: float = 0.42) -> bool:
   186:     if not phrase or not target:
   187:         return False
   188: 
   189:     title = target.get("title") or target.get("label") or target.get("h1") or ""
   190:     if not title:
   191:         return False
   192: 
   193:     if target.get("source_type") != "document_registry":
   194:         return False
   195:

   197: 
   198: 
   199: def runtime_safe_payload(phrase: str, target: Dict[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, Any]:
   200:     score = normalized_target_score(phrase, target)
   201:     allowed = semantic_gate_allows(phrase, target)
   202: 
   203:     url = target.get("url") if allowed else ""
   204: 
   205:     return {
   206:         "anchor": phrase,
   207:         "url": url or "",
   208:         "title": target.get("title") or "",
   209:         "label": target.get("label") or target.get("title") or "",
   210:         "source_type": "document_registry",
   211:         "source_origin": target.get("source_origin") or "uploaded_editor_documents",
   212:         "score": score,
   213:         "allowed": allowed,
   214:         "document_id": target.get("document_id") or "",
   215:         "cross_document_linking": bool(target.get("cross_document_linking")),
   216:         "is_placeholder_url": bool(target.get("is_placeholder_url")),
   217:         "placeholder_url": target.get("placeholder_url") or "",
   218:         "published_url": target.get("published_url") or "",
   219:         "diagnostics": {

   233:     phrase: str,

   238:     targets = load_document_registry_targets(workspace_id)
   239:     ranked: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []

   242:         if exclude_document_id and str(target.get("document_id") or "") == str(exclude_document_id):
   243:             continue
   244: 
   245:         payload = runtime_safe_payload(phrase, target)
   246:         if payload["allowed"]:
   247:             ranked.append(payload)
   248: 
   249:     ranked.sort(key=lambda x: x.get("score", 0), reverse=True)

    40: def _dedupe(urls: List[str]) -> List[str]:
    41:     seen = set()
    42:     out: List[str] = []
    43:     for u in urls:
    44:         u = (u or "").strip()
    45:         if not u:
    46:             continue
    47:         if u not in seen:
    48:             seen.add(u)
    49:             out.append(u)
    50:     return out
    51:

    53:     s = (s or "").strip()
    54:     return s.startswith("http://") or s.startswith("https://")

   164: async def _extract_upload_from_request(request: Request) -> Tuple[Optional[str], bytes]:
   165:     """
   166:     Accepts:
   167:       - multipart/form-data with many possible file field names
   168:       - raw body (text/plain, text/csv, application/xml, etc.)
   169:     Returns (filename, bytes).
   170:     """
   171:     ct = (request.headers.get("content-type") or "").lower()
   172: 
   173:     def _is_uploadfile_like(v: Any) -> bool:
   174:         # Starlette/FastAPI UploadFile-like: has .filename and async .read()
   175:         return hasattr(v, "filename") and hasattr(v, "read")
   176: 
   177:     # multipart
   178:     if "multipart/form-data" in ct:
   179:         form = await request.form()
   180: 
   181:         # common keys first
   182:         for key in ("file", "upload", "uploads", "files", "files[]", "sitemap", "sitemapFile"):
   183:             v = form.get(key)
   184:             if v is None:
   185:                 continue
   186: 
   187:             # if multiple files
   188:             if isinstance(v, list) and v:
   189:                 v = v[0]
   190: 
   191:             # UploadFile-like
   192:             if _is_uploadfile_like(v):
   193:                 data = await v.read()
   194:                 return (getattr(v, "filename", None) or "upload.bin"), (data or b"")
   195: 
   196:             # sometimes frameworks put raw bytes/string in the form
   197:             if isinstance(v, (bytes, bytearray)):
   198:                 return ("upload.bin", bytes(v))
   199:             if isinstance(v, str) and v.strip():
   200:                 return ("upload.txt", v.encode("utf-8"))
   201: 
   202:         # fallback: grab the first UploadFile-like entry anywhere in form
   203:         try:
   204:             items = form.multi_items()
   205:         except Exception:
   206:             items = list(form.items())
   207: 
   208:         for _, v in items:
   209:             if isinstance(v, list) and v:
   210:                 v = v[0]
   211: 
   212:             if _is_uploadfile_like(v):
   213:                 data = await v.read()
   214:                 return (getattr(v, "filename", None) or "upload.bin"), (data or b"")
   215: 
   216:             if isinstance(v, (bytes, bytearray)):
   217:                 return ("upload.bin", bytes(v))
   218:             if isinstance(v, str) and v.strip():
   219:                 return ("upload.txt", v.encode("utf-8"))
   220: 
   221:         return (None, b"")
   222: 
   223:     # raw body
   224:     data = await request.body()
   225:     return ("upload.bin", data or b"")
   226:

   250: @router.post("/upload")
   251: async def upload_urls(
   252:     request: Request,
   253:     workspace_id: Optional[str] = Query(None),
   254:     workspaceId: Optional[str] = Query(None),
   255: ):
   256:     """
   257:     Frontend legacy upload -> writes to WS-only imported_targets_<ws>.<ext>.
   258:     Supports multipart OR raw body.
   259:     """
   260:     ws = _pick_ws(workspace_id, workspaceId)
   261: 
   262:     filename, data = await _extract_upload_from_request(request)
   263:     if not data:
   264:         raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="Empty upload")
   265: 
   266:     _ensure_data_dir()
   267:     p = _paths(ws)
   268: 
   269:     ext = _infer_ext(filename or "", request.headers.get("content-type") or "")
   270:     out_path = p.get(ext)
   271:     if not out_path:
   272:         raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="Unsupported file type")
   273: 
   274:     try:
   275:         with open(out_path, "wb") as f:
   276:             f.write(data)
   277:     except Exception as e:
   278:         raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail=f"Failed to save: {e}")
   279: 
   280:     after = _load_all(ws)
   281:     return {
   282:         "ok": True,
   283:         "build": IMPORTS_COMPAT_BUILD,
   284:         "workspace_id": ws,
   285:         "saved_to": out_path,

MATCH LINE: 251
PATTERN: def upload
CODE: async def upload_urls(
------------------------------------------------------------
   227: 
   228: def _infer_ext(filename: str, content_type: str) -> str:
   229:     fn = (filename or "").lower().strip()
   230: 
   231:     if fn.endswith(".csv"):
   232:         return "csv"
   233:     if fn.endswith(".txt"):
   234:         return "txt"
   235:     if fn.endswith(".xml"):
   236:         return "xml"
   237: 
   238:     ct = (content_type or "").lower()
   239:     if "text/csv" in ct:
   240:         return "csv"
   241:     if "application/xml" in ct or "text/xml" in ct:
   242:         return "xml"
   243:     if "text/plain" in ct:
   244:         return "txt"
   245: 
   246:     # default safest
   247:     return "txt"
   248: 
   249: 
   250: @router.post("/upload")
   251: async def upload_urls(
   252:     request: Request,
   253:     workspace_id: Optional[str] = Query(None),
   254:     workspaceId: Optional[str] = Query(None),
   255: ):
   256:     """
   257:     Frontend legacy upload -> writes to WS-only imported_targets_<ws>.<ext>.
   258:     Supports multipart OR raw body.
   259:     """
   260:     ws = _pick_ws(workspace_id, workspaceId)
   261: 
   262:     filename, data = await _extract_upload_from_request(request)
   263:     if not data:
   264:         raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="Empty upload")
   265: 
   266:     _ensure_data_dir()
   267:     p = _paths(ws)
   268: 
   269:     ext = _infer_ext(filename or "", request.headers.get("content-type") or "")
   270:     out_path = p.get(ext)
   271:     if not out_path:
   272:         raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="Unsupported file type")
   273: 
   274:     try:
   275:         with open(out_path, "wb") as f:
   276:             f.write(data)
   277:     except Exception as e:
   278:         raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail=f"Failed to save: {e}")
   279: 
   280:     after = _load_all(ws)
   281:     return {
   282:         "ok": True,
   283:         "build": IMPORTS_COMPAT_BUILD,
   284:         "workspace_id": ws,
   285:         "saved_to": out_path,
   286:         "counts": after["counts"],
   287:         "total_unique": after["counts"]["total_unique"],
   288:     }
   289: 
   290: 
   291: # Alias: some frontend code uses /api/urls/import instead of /upload

PATTERN: active_target_set
CODE: active = _load_active_target_set(workspace_id)
------------------------------------------------------------
   198:     """
   199:     Step 3: Semantic Target Discovery.
   200: 
   201:     Responsibilities:
   202:     - Read only Step 2C-ready phrases.
   203:     - Load Active Target Set.
   204:     - Match semantic_match_candidates against target records.
   205:     - Return ranked candidate URLs with evidence.
   206: 
   207:     Non-responsibilities:
   208:     - Does not crawl.
   209:     - Does not learn semantic meaning.
   210:     - Does not make final link decision.
   211:     - Does not insert editor links.
   212:     """
   213: 
   214:     if not understood_phrase.get("ready_for_target_discovery"):
   215:         return {
   216:             "ok": False,
   217:             "error": "phrase_not_ready_for_target_discovery",
   218:             "phrase_id": understood_phrase.get("phrase_id"),
   219:             "candidate_targets": [],
   220:         }
   221: 
   222:     active = _load_active_target_set(workspace_id)
   223:     if not active.get("ok"):
   224:         return {
   225:             "ok": False,
   226:             "error": active.get("error"),
   227:             "phrase_id": understood_phrase.get("phrase_id"),
   228:             "workspace_id": workspace_id,
   229:             "candidate_targets": [],
   230:             "active_target_set_path": active.get("path"),
   231:         }
   232: 
   233:     semantic_candidates = understood_phrase.get("semantic_match_candidates") or []
   234:     targets = active.get("items") or []
   235: 
   236:     ranked: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []
   237: 
   238:     for target in targets:
   239:         best_score = 0.0
   240:         best_evidence: Optional[Dict[str, Any]] = None
   241: 
   242:         for candidate in semantic_candidates:
   243:             result = _score_candidate_against_target(candidate, target)
   244:             if result["score"] > best_score:
   245:                 best_score = result["score"]
   246:                 best_evidence = result["evidence"]
   247: 
   248:         if best_score > 0:
   249:             ranked.append({
   250:                 "url": target.get("url"),
   251:                 "title": target.get("title"),
   252:                 "slug": target.get("slug"),
   253:                 "topic_cluster": target.get("topic_cluster"),
   254:                 "section_cluster": target.get("section_cluster"),
   255:                 "source_pool": target.get("source_pool"),
   256:                 "retrieval_score": round(best_score, 4),
   257:                 "candidate_evidence": best_evidence or {},
   258:                 "target_record": target,
   259:             })
   260: 
   261:     ranked.sort(key=lambda x: x.get("retrieval_score", 0), reverse=True)
   262:     ranked = ranked[: max(1, int(limit or 10))]
   263: 
   264:     output_phrase = dict(understood_phrase)
   265:     output_phrase["candidate_target_urls"] = ranked
   266:     output_phrase["processing_state"] = {
   267:         **(output_phrase.get("processing_state") or {}),
   268:         "current_engine": "semantic_target_discovery",
   269:         "status": "target_candidates_found" if ranked else "no_target_candidates_found",
   270:         "target_candidate_count": len(ranked),
   271:         "next_expected_engine": "yellow_semantic_resolver" if ranked else None,
   272:     }
   273:     output_phrase["next_expected_engine"] = "yellow_semantic_resolver" if ranked else None
   274: 
   275:     return {
   276:         "ok": True,
   277:         "schema_version": "semantic_target_discovery_model_v1",
   278:         "workspace_id": workspace_id,

   223:     if not active.get("ok"):
   224:         return {
   225:             "ok": False,
   226:             "error": active.get("error"),
   227:             "phrase_id": understood_phrase.get("phrase_id"),
   228:             "workspace_id": workspace_id,
   229:             "candidate_targets": [],
   230:             "active_target_set_path": active.get("path"),
   231:         }
   232: 
   233:     semantic_candidates = understood_phrase.get("semantic_match_candidates") or []
   234:     targets = active.get("items") or []
   235: 
   236:     ranked: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []
   237: 
   238:     for target in targets:
   239:         best_score = 0.0
   240:         best_evidence: Optional[Dict[str, Any]] = None
   241: 
   242:         for candidate in semantic_candidates:
   243:             result = _score_candidate_against_target(candidate, target)
   244:             if result["score"] > best_score:
   245:                 best_score = result["score"]
   246:                 best_evidence = result["evidence"]
   247: 
   248:         if best_score > 0:
   249:             ranked.append({
   250:                 "url": target.get("url"),
   251:                 "title": target.get("title"),
   252:                 "slug": target.get("slug"),
   253:                 "topic_cluster": target.get("topic_cluster"),
   254:                 "section_cluster": target.get("section_cluster"),
   255:                 "source_pool": target.get("source_pool"),
   256:                 "retrieval_score": round(best_score, 4),
   257:                 "candidate_evidence": best_evidence or {},
   258:                 "target_record": target,
   259:             })
   260: 
   261:     ranked.sort(key=lambda x: x.get("retrieval_score", 0), reverse=True)
   262:     ranked = ranked[: max(1, int(limit or 10))]
   263: 
   264:     output_phrase = dict(understood_phrase)
   265:     output_phrase["candidate_target_urls"] = ranked
   266:     output_phrase["processing_state"] = {
   267:         **(output_phrase.get("processing_state") or {}),
   268:         "current_engine": "semantic_target_discovery",
   269:         "status": "target_candidates_found" if ranked else "no_target_candidates_found",
   270:         "target_candidate_count": len(ranked),
   271:         "next_expected_engine": "yellow_semantic_resolver" if ranked else None,
   272:     }
   273:     output_phrase["next_expected_engine"] = "yellow_semantic_resolver" if ranked else None
   274: 
   275:     return {
   276:         "ok": True,
   277:         "schema_version": "semantic_target_discovery_model_v1",
   278:         "workspace_id": workspace_id,
   279:         "active_target_set_path": active.get("path"),
   280:         "phrase_id": understood_phrase.get("phrase_id"),
   281:         "phrase_text": understood_phrase.get("phrase_text") or understood_phrase.get("text"),

   606: 
   607: 
   608: def _canonical_url_governance_multiplier(
   609:     row: Dict[str, Any],
   610: ) -> float:
   611: 
   612:     url = row.get("url", "")
   613:     source_type = str(
   614:         row.get("source_type") or ""
   615:     ).strip().lower()
   616: 
   617:     # Published/live URLs should dominate canonical SEO selection.
   618:     if source_type == "live_domain":
   619:         return 1.35
   620: 
   621:     # Imported URLs are usually external/published references.
   622:     if source_type == "imported":
   623:         return 1.15
   624: 
   625:     # Drafts are useful but should not dominate published URLs.
   626:     if source_type == "draft":
   627:         return 0.82
   628: 
   629:     # Internal document registry should almost never be canonical SEO.
   630:     if source_type == "document_registry":
   631:         return 0.28
   632: 
   633:     if _is_internal_noncanonical_url(url):
   634:         return 0.20
   635: 
   636:     return 1.0
   637: 
   638: 
   639: def _canonical_url_penalty_reason(
   640:     row: Dict[str, Any],
   641: ) -> str:
   642: 
   643:     url = row.get("url", "")
   644:     source_type = str(
   645:         row.get("source_type") or ""
   646:     ).strip().lower()
   647: 
   648:     if source_type == "live_domain":
   649:         return "published_live_domain_priority"
   650: 
   651:     if source_type == "imported":
   652:         return "imported_published_reference_priority"
   653: 
   654:     if source_type == "draft":
   655:         return "draft_candidate_lower_than_published"
   656: 
   657:     if source_type == "document_registry":
   658:         return "internal_document_registry_not_seo_canonical"
   659: 
   660:     if _is_internal_noncanonical_url(url):
   661:         return "internal_noncanonical_url_penalty"
   662: 
   663:     return "standard_canonical_candidate"
   664:

   430: async def convert_docx(file: UploadFile = File(...)):
   431: 
   432: 
   433:     if file is None or not (file.filename or "").lower().endswith(".docx"):
   434: 
   435: 
   436:         raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="Please upload a .docx file.")
   437: 
   438: 
   439:     try:
   440: 
   441: 
   442:         data = await file.read()
   443: 
   444: 
   445:         with io.BytesIO(data) as buff:
   446: 
   447: 
   448:             result = mammoth.convert_to_html(buff)
   449: 
   450: 
   451:             html = result.value or ""
   452: 
   453: 
   454:         try:
   455: 
   456: 
   457:             with io.BytesIO(data) as buff2:
   458: 
   459: 
   460:                 raw = mammoth.extract_raw_text(buff2)
   461: 
   462: 
   463:                 text = raw.value or ""
   464: 
   465: 
   466:         except Exception:
   467: 
   468: 
   469:             text = ""
   470: 
   471: 
   472:         return {"ok": True, "filename": file.filename, "ext": ".docx", "html": html, "text": text}
   473: 
   474: 
   475:     except Exception as e:
   476: 
   477: 
   478:         raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail=f"DOCX conversion error: {e}")
   479:

    37: def _read_json(path: Path, default: Any) -> Any:
    38:     try:
    39:         if path.exists():
    40:             return json.loads(path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    41:     except Exception:
    42:         pass
    43:     return default

    46: def _write_json(path: Path, obj: Any) -> Any:
    47:     path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    48:     path.write_text(json.dumps(obj, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2), encoding="utf-8")
    49:     return obj
    50:

    52: def _unique_clean(urls: List[str]) -> List[str]:
    53:     seen = set()
    54:     out = []
    55:     for raw in urls or []:
    56:         url = str(raw or "").strip()
    57:         if not url or url in seen:
    58:             continue
    59:         seen.add(url)
    60:         out.append(url)
    61:     return out
    62: 
    63: 
    64: def _domain_from_url(url: str) -> str:
    65:     try:
    66:         u = urlparse((url or "").strip())
    67:         return (u.netloc or "").strip().lower().split(":")[0]
    68:     except Exception:
    69:         return ""

    73:     """
    74:     URL Pool Manager safety classification.
    75: 
    76:     This is NOT relevance scoring.
    77:     This only classifies whether a URL is allowed into URL pool lifecycle.
    78:     """
    79:     u = str(url or "").strip()
    80:     if not u:
    81:         return {"decision": "reject", "class": "reject", "reason": "empty_url"}
    82: 
    83:     lower_url = u.lower()
    84:     path = urlparse(u).path or ""
    85:     path_lower = path.lower().strip()

   194: def load_all_sitemap_urls(workspace_id: str) -> List[str]:
   195:     """
   196:     Returns full usable URL inventory from site_sources.
   197: 
   198:     This is for URL Pool Manager lifecycle:
   199:     - active URLs are in active_target_set
   200:     - reserve URLs come from full sitemap inventory minus active URLs
   201:     - this function only applies safety cleanup, not scoring or ranking
   202:     """
   203:     obj = _read_json(_site_sources_path(workspace_id), {})
   204:     urls: List[str] = []
   205: 
   206:     for source in obj.get("sources") or []:
   207:         if not isinstance(source, dict):
   208:             continue
   209:         for u in source.get("sitemap_urls") or []:
   210:             if str(u).strip() and _is_safe_reserve_url(str(u).strip(), workspace_id):
   211:                 urls.append(str(u).strip())
   212: 
   213:     return _unique_clean(urls)
   214:

   216: def load_active_urls(workspace_id: str) -> List[str]:
   217:     obj = _read_json(_active_target_set_path(workspace_id), {})
   218:     return _unique_clean(obj.get("active_live_domain_urls") or [])
   219: 
   220: 
   221: def save_active_urls(workspace_id: str, urls: List[str]) -> Dict[str, Any]:
   222:     path = _active_target_set_path(workspace_id)
   223:     obj = _read_json(path, {})
   224: 
   225:     if not isinstance(obj, dict):
   226:         obj = {}
   227: 
   228:     obj["workspace_id"] = workspace_id
   229:     obj["active_live_domain_urls"] = _unique_clean(urls)
   230:     obj["updated_at"] = _now()
   231: 
   232:     obj.setdefault("active_document_ids", [])
   233:     obj.setdefault("active_draft_ids", [])
   234:     obj.setdefault("active_imported_urls", [])
   235: 
   236:     return _write_json(path, obj)
   237:

   593: def _host_matches_domain(url: str, domain: str) -> bool:
   594:     try:
   595:         host = urlparse(str(url or "")).netloc.lower().strip()
   596:         host = host[4:] if host.startswith("www.") else host
   597:         dom = str(domain or "").lower().strip()
   598:         dom = dom[4:] if dom.startswith("www.") else dom
   599:         return bool(host and dom and (host == dom or host.endswith("." + dom)))
   600:     except Exception:
   601:         return False

   604: def build_imported_target_pool(
   605:     workspace_id: str,
   606:     *,
   607:     placeholder_path: str = "/drafts",
   608:     merge_all: bool = True,
   609: ) -> Dict[str, Any]:
   610:     """
   611:     Imported Target Pool (WS ONLY, formats ONLY):
   612:       - Reads ONLY ws-scoped inputs: imported_targets_<ws>.(csv|txt|xml)
   613:       - Topic-only rows become placeholder URLs under placeholder_path.
   614:       - Dedup by URL; prefer an explicit title/label when duplicates exist.
   615:       - Output items: {url, label}
   616:       - if active_target_set_<ws>.json exists, only keeps active imported URLs
   617:     """
   618:     ws = _clean(workspace_id)
   619:     if not ws:
   620:         raise ValueError("workspace_id is required")
   621: 
   622:     domain = _domain_from_sources_or_ws(ws)
   623:     base = f"https://{domain}".rstrip("/")
   624:     pp = "/" + str(placeholder_path or "/drafts").lstrip("/")
   625:     pp = pp.rstrip("/")
   626: 
   627:     sources_used: List[str] = []
   628:     per_source_counts: Dict[str, int] = {}
   629:     all_pairs: List[Tuple[str, str]] = []
   630: 
   631:     def add_pairs(name: str, pairs: List[Tuple[str, str]]) -> None:
   632:         if not pairs:
   633:             return
   634:         sources_used.append(name)
   635:         per_source_counts[name] = len(pairs)
   636:         all_pairs.extend(pairs)
   637: 
   638:     csv_fp = _csv_path(ws)
   639:     txt_fp = _txt_path(ws)
   640:     xml_fp = _xml_path(ws)
   641: 
   642:     if merge_all:
   643:         if csv_fp.exists():
   644:             add_pairs(str(csv_fp), _read_csv(csv_fp))
   645:         if txt_fp.exists():
   646:             add_pairs(str(txt_fp), _read_txt(txt_fp))
   647:         if xml_fp.exists():
   648:             add_pairs(str(xml_fp), _read_xml_sitemap(xml_fp))
   649:     else:
   650:         if csv_fp.exists():
   651:             add_pairs(str(csv_fp), _read_csv(csv_fp))
   652:         elif txt_fp.exists():
   653:             add_pairs(str(txt_fp), _read_txt(txt_fp))
   654:         elif xml_fp.exists():
   655:             add_pairs(str(xml_fp), _read_xml_sitemap(xml_fp))
   656: 
   657:     if not all_pairs:
   658:         raise FileNotFoundError(
   659:             "No imported target inputs found (or all were empty). Looked for: "
   660:             f"{csv_fp.name}, {txt_fp.name}, {xml_fp.name} in backend/server/data/"
   661:         )

   671: 
   672:     active_imported_url_set = set(active_imported_urls)

   216:     for p in paragraphs:
   217:         ordered.append(
   218:             (
   219:                 int(p.get("start_char") or 0),
   220:                 {
   221:                     "type": "paragraph",
   222:                     "index": p.get("index"),
   223:                     "text_preview": str(p.get("text") or "")[:160],
   224:                     "start_char": p.get("start_char"),
   225:                     "word_count": p.get("word_count"),
   226:                 },
   227:             )
   228:         )
   229: 
   230:     # Headings sort just before the paragraph that contains them (same
   231:     # position): stable sort with heading entries added first achieves that.
   232:     ordered.sort(key=lambda t: t[0])
   233:     document_order = [item for _, item in ordered]
   234: 
   235:     word_count = len([w for w in re.split(r"\s+", str(content_body or "")) if w.strip()])
   236: 
   237:     return {
   238:         "paragraphs": paragraphs,
   239:         "heading_map": heading_map,
   240:         "section_count": len(heading_map),
   241:         "paragraph_count": len(paragraphs),
   242:         "document_order": document_order,
   243: 
   244:         "first_heading": headings[0] if headings else "",
   245:         "last_heading": headings[-1] if headings else "",
   246:         "first_paragraph": paragraphs[0]["text"] if paragraphs else "",
   247:         "last_paragraph": paragraphs[-1]["text"] if paragraphs else "",
   248:         "estimated_word_count": word_count,
   249:         "estimated_character_count": len(str(content_body or "")),

   261: def build_uduc_from_upload_extraction_result(
   262:     *,
   263:     extraction_result: Any,
   264:     workspace_id: str,
   265:     document_id: str | None = None,
   266:     original_filename: str | None = None,
   267:     stored_filename: str | None = None,
   268:     stored_path: str | None = None,
   269:     source_metadata: Optional[Dict[str, Any]] = None,
   270: ) -> UploadedDocumentUnifiedContent:
   271:     """
   272:     Convert UploadExtractionResult into Uploaded Document Unified Content.
   273: 
   274:     Boundary rules:
   275:     - Does not extract documents.
   276:     - Does not clean or rewrite content_body.
   277:     - Does not perform phrase extraction.
   278:     - Does not perform semantic analysis.
   279:     - Does not build UUCD.
   280:     """
   281: 
   282:     ws = _safe_workspace_id(workspace_id)
   283: 
   284:     if isinstance(extraction_result, dict):
   285:         er = extraction_result
   286:     else:
   287:         er = {
   288:             "source_path": getattr(extraction_result, "source_path", ""),
   289:             "source_type": getattr(extraction_result, "source_type", ""),
   290:             "title": getattr(extraction_result, "title", ""),
   291:             "text": getattr(extraction_result, "text", ""),
   292:             "headings": getattr(extraction_result, "headings", []),
   293:             "metadata": getattr(extraction_result, "metadata", {}),
   294:             "extraction_status": getattr(extraction_result, "extraction_status", ""),
   295:             "extraction_confidence": getattr(extraction_result, "extraction_confidence", 0.0),
   296:             "created_at": getattr(extraction_result, "created_at", ""),
   297:         }
   298: 
   299:     meta = er.get("metadata") if isinstance(er.get("metadata"), dict) else {}
   300:     src_meta = dict(source_metadata or {})
   301: 
   302:     inferred_document_id = (
   303:         document_id
   304:         or src_meta.get("doc_id")
   305:         or src_meta.get("document_id")
   306:         or meta.get("doc_id")
   307:         or meta.get("document_id")
   308:         or ""
   309:     )
   310: 
   311:     doc_id = _safe_document_id(inferred_document_id)
   312: 
   313:     index_hit = _read_upload_index_hit(ws, doc_id) if doc_id != "unknown_document" else {}
   314: 
   315:     source_path = str(er.get("source_path") or stored_path or index_hit.get("stored_path") or "")
   316:     source_type = str(er.get("source_type") or meta.get("source_type") or "").strip()
   317:     source_format = source_type or str(meta.get("extension") or "").replace(".", "").strip() or "uploaded_document"

    36:     except Exception:
    37:         return default

   422:         }
   423: 
   424:         if state == "completed":
   425:             completed_documents.append(row)
   426:         elif state in {"failed", "dead_letter"}:
   427:             failed_documents.append(row)
   428:         else:
   429:             pending_documents.append(row)
   430: 
   431:     all_terminal = all(
   432:         (read_job_status(ws, j.get("job_id", "")).get("status") or j.get("status")) in terminal_statuses
   433:         for j in document_jobs
   434:     ) if document_jobs else False
   435: 
   436:     all_completed = document_count > 0 and len(completed_documents) == document_count
   437:     has_failures = len(failed_documents) > 0
   438:     has_pending = len(pending_documents) > 0

    59: // NOTE: This now uploads every file type to backend so it is persisted + indexed for pools.
    60: // It still reads some formats locally only to keep a smooth preview, but the source of truth is backend.
    61: export async function uploadFile(file, workspaceId) {
    62:   if (!file || !file.name) throw new Error("No file provided");
    63: 
    64:   const ws = getWorkspaceId(workspaceId);
    65:   const ext = extOf(file.name);
    66: 
    67:   // Store original blob locally for "Download original"
    68:   ORIGINAL_BLOBS.set(file.name, file);
    69: 
    70:   // Always upload to backend so it lands in:
    71:   // backend/server/data/docs/<ws>/
    72:   const fd = new FormData();
    73:   fd.append("file", file);
    74: 
    75:   const res = await fetch(`${API_BASE}/api/files/upload?workspace_id=${encodeURIComponent(ws)}`, {
    76:     method: "POST",
    77:     body: fd,
    78:   });
    79: 
    80:   let data;
    81:   try {
    82:     data = await res.json();
    83:   } catch {
    84:     throw new Error(`upload_failed_http_${res.status}`);
    85:   }
    86: 
    87:   if (!res.ok || !data || data.ok !== true) {
    88:     const msg =
    89:       data && (data.detail || data.error)
    90:         ? (data.detail || data.error)
    91:         : `upload_failed_http_${res.status}`;
    92:     throw new Error(msg);
    93:   }
    94: 
    95:   // Backend returns:
    96:   // { ok, workspace_id, doc:{...}, filename, ext, text, html, is_html, truncated }
    97:   const doc = data.doc || {};
    98:   const text = (data.text ?? doc.text ?? "") || "";
    99:   const html = (data.html ?? doc.html ?? "") || "";
   100: 
   101:   return asDocRecord({
   102:     filename: data.filename || doc.filename || file.name,
   103:     ext: data.ext || doc.ext || ext,
   104:     text,
   105:     html,
   106:     size: file.size,
   107:     doc_id: doc.doc_id || "",
   108:     h1: doc.h1 || "",
   109:     h1_source: doc.h1_source || "",
   110:     h1_error: doc.h1_error || "",
   111:     workspace_id: data.workspace_id || ws,
   112:   });
   113: }
   114: 
   115: // -------------------------
   116: // Downloads / Exports
   117: // -------------------------

   118: export function downloadOriginalUrl(filename) {
   119:   if (ORIGINAL_BLOBS.has(filename)) {
   120:     try {
   121:       if (ORIGINAL_URLS.has(filename)) {
   122:         URL.revokeObjectURL(ORIGINAL_URLS.get(filename));
   123:       }
   124:     } catch {}
   125:     const url = URL.createObjectURL(ORIGINAL_BLOBS.get(filename));
   126:     ORIGINAL_URLS.set(filename, url);
   127:     return url;
   128:   }

     6: async function apiFetch(path, opts = {}) {
     7:   const res = await fetch(path, opts);
     8:   if (!res.ok) {
     9:     const msg = await res.text().catch(() => "");
    10:     throw new Error(msg || `HTTP ${res.status}`);
    11:   }
    12:   return res.json();
    13: }
    14: 
    15: export async function loadImportedUrls(workspaceId = DEFAULT_WORKSPACE) {
    16:   const data = await apiFetch(`/api/urls/list?workspace_id=${encodeURIComponent(workspaceId)}`);
    17:   IMPORTED_URLS = new Set(Array.isArray(data.urls) ? data.urls : []);

    31: export async function importUrlsFromFile(file, workspaceId = DEFAULT_WORKSPACE) {
    32:   const fd = new FormData();
    33:   fd.append("file", file);
    34:

    36:     method: "POST",
    37:     body: fd,
    38:   });
    39: 
    40:   // refresh local cache
    41:   await loadImportedUrls(workspaceId);
    42: 
    43:   return Number(data.added || 0);
    44: }
    45: 
    46: /**
    47:  * Back-compat (if anything still calls these):
    48:  * We keep names, but route them to backend by creating a Blob File.
    49:  */
    50: function textToFile(text, filename) {
    51:   const blob = new Blob([String(text || "")], { type: "text/plain" });
    52:   return new File([blob], filename || "import.txt", { type: "text/plain" });
    53: }
    54:

    14: # -------------------------
    15: def _data_dir() -> str:
    16:     # backend/server/data
    17:     return os.path.join(os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(__file__)), "data")
    18: 
    19: def _ws_safe(ws: str) -> str:
    20:     ws = (ws or "default").strip().lower()
    21:     return re.sub(r"[^a-z0-9_\-]", "_", ws)[:80] or "default"
    22: 
    23: def _path_for_workspace(workspace_id: str) -> str:
    24:     ws = _ws_safe(workspace_id)
    25:     return os.path.join(_data_dir(), f"draft_topics_{ws}.json")
    26: 
    27: def _meta_path_for_workspace(workspace_id: str) -> str:
    28:     ws = _ws_safe(workspace_id)
    29:     return os.path.join(_data_dir(), f"draft_topics_{ws}.meta.json")
    30: 
    31: def _safe_read_json(path: str) -> Any:
    32:     try:
    33:         with open(path, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
    34:             return json.load(f)
    35:     except FileNotFoundError:
    36:         return None
    37:     except Exception:
    38:         return None
    39: 
    40: def _atomic_write_json(path: str, data: Any) -> None:
    41:     os.makedirs(os.path.dirname(path), exist_ok=True)
    42:     tmp = path + ".tmp"
    43:     with open(tmp, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
    44:         json.dump(data, f, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2)
    45:     os.replace(tmp, path)
    46: 
    47: def _write_meta(workspace_id: str, filename: str, added: int, updated: int, total: int, data_path: str) -> None:
    48:     meta_path = _meta_path_for_workspace(workspace_id)
    49:     payload = {
    50:         "workspace_id": _ws_safe(workspace_id),
    51:         "last_import_at_utc": datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat(),
    52:         "last_import_filename": filename or "",
    53:         "last_import_added": int(added or 0),
    54:         "last_import_updated": int(updated or 0),
    55:         "total_topics": int(total or 0),
    56:         "data_path": data_path,  # OK for local dev; later on AWS you can make this relative
    57:     }

   245:             continue
   246:         if tid in index:
   247:             index[tid].update(item)

   249:         else:
   250:             index[tid] = item
   251:             added += 1

   261:         "workspace_id": _ws_safe(workspace_id),
   262:         "added": added,
   263:         "updated": updated,
   264:         "total": len(merged),
   265:     }

    45: def _now_iso() -> str:
    46:     return datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat()
    47: 
    48: 
    49: def _normalize_domain(value: str | None) -> str:
    50:     raw = str(value or "").strip().lower()
    51: 
    52:     if not raw:
    53:         raise HTTPException(
    54:             status_code=400,
    55:             detail="domain or url is required",
    56:         )
    57: 
    58:     raw = re.sub(r"^https?://", "", raw, flags=re.IGNORECASE)
    59:     raw = raw.split("/", 1)[0].strip()
    60:     raw = raw.rstrip(".")
    61: 
    62:     if raw.startswith("www."):
    63:         raw = raw[4:]
    64: 
    65:     if not raw or "." not in raw:
    66:         raise HTTPException(
    67:             status_code=400,
    68:             detail="Invalid domain",
    69:         )
    70: 
    71:     return raw
    72: 
    73: 
    74: def _workspace_id_from_domain(domain: str) -> str:
    75:     normalized = re.sub(
    76:         r"[^a-z0-9]+",
    77:         "_",
    78:         str(domain or "").lower(),
    79:     ).strip("_")
    80: 
    81:     if not normalized:
    82:         raise HTTPException(
    83:             status_code=400,
    84:             detail="Could not derive workspace_id from domain",
    85:         )
    86: 
    87:     return f"ws_{normalized}"
    88: 
    89: 
    90: def _resolve_workspace_id(
    91:     workspace_id: str | None,
    92:     domain: str,
    93: ) -> str:
    94:     supplied = str(workspace_id or "").strip()
    95: 
    96:     if supplied:
    97:         if not supplied.startswith("ws_"):

  1117:         showToast("Upload documents first");
  1118:         return;
  1119:       }

  1121:       const titles = docs.map(d => {
  1122:         const base = String(d.name || "").replace(/\.[a-z0-9]+$/i, "");
  1123:         const clean = base.replace(/[_-]+/g, " ").replace(/\s+/g, " ").trim();
  1124:         return clean || "Untitled Document";
  1125:       });
  1126:

  1128:       const seen = new Set();

  1132:         if (seen.has(k)) continue;
  1133:         seen.add(k);
  1134:         uniq.push(t);
  1135:       }
  1136:

  1139:         title: t
  1140:       }));
  1141: 
  1142:       // Ensure per-pillar counts exist
  1143:       for (const p of newP){
  1144:         if (!state.clusterCountByPillar[p.id]){
  1145:           state.clusterCountByPillar[p.id] = getSettings().defaultClusterCount || 23;
  1146:         }
  1147:       }
  1148:       saveState();
  1149: 
  1150:       setPillars(newP);
  1151:       applyAutoRules();
  1152:       renderAll();
  1153:       showToast("Pillars derived from documents");
  1154:     }

   106:         "explaining topics",
   107:         "influence learning performance",
   108:         "another essential learning strategy",
   109:     }
   110: 
   111:     saved_phrases = set(built.get("phrases", {}).keys())
   112:     false_positives = sorted(saved_phrases & false_positive_terms)
   113: 
   114:     expected_good = {
   115:         "study plan",
   116:         "effective study plan",
   117:         "active learning methods",
   118:         "mock exams",
   119:         "past questions",
   120:         "study environment",
   121:         "practice questions",
   122:         "academic assessment",
   123:     }
   124: 
   125:     good_found = sorted(saved_phrases & expected_good)
   126:     good_missing = sorted(expected_good - saved_phrases)
   127: 
   128:     score = 10.0
   129:     score -= len(false_positives) * 0.35
   130:     score -= len(good_missing) * 0.60
   131:     score = max(0.0, round(score, 1))
   132: 
   133:     production_ready = score >= 9.0 and not false_positives and not good_missing
   134: 
   135:     print()
   136:     print("===== RATING / PRODUCTION READINESS =====")
   137:     print("rating:", f"{score}/10")
   138:     print("production_ready:", production_ready)
   139:     print("good_found:", good_found)
   140:     print("good_missing:", good_missing)
   141:     print("false_positives:", false_positives)

   781: def build_upload_phrase_pool(ws: str) -> Dict[str, Any]:
   782:     ws = _ws_safe(ws)
   783: 
   784:     src_path = _upload_phrase_index_path(ws)
   785:     pool_path = _upload_phrase_pool_path(ws)
   786: 
   787:     src = _safe_read_json(src_path) or {}
   788:     raw_phrases = src.get("phrases") if isinstance(src.get("phrases"), dict) else {}
   789: 
   790:     phrases: Dict[str, Any] = {}
   791: 
   792:     for k, v in raw_phrases.items():
   793:         nk = _clean_text(k)
   794: 
   795:         if not nk or not isinstance(v, dict):
   796:             continue
   797: 
   798:         clean_rec = dict(v)
   799:         source_type = _clean_text(clean_rec.get("source_type") or "")
   800: 
   801:         phrase_text = _light_normalize_phrase(_clean_text(clean_rec.get("phrase") or nk))
   802:         if not phrase_text:
   803:             continue
   804: 
   805:         gate_result = _quality_gate_phrase_with_metadata(
   806:             phrase_text,
   807:             source_type=source_type or "",
   808:             snippet=_extract_record_snippet(clean_rec),
   809:         )
   810: 
   811:         gate_reason = str(gate_result.get("reason") or "")
   812: 
   813:         hard_pool_reject = gate_reason in {
   814:             "malformed_wrapper",
   815:             "malformed_wrapper_validation_failed",
   816:             "orphan_tail",
   817:             "orphan_tail_start",
   818:             "boundary_spillover",
   819:             "prefix_suffix_spillover",
   820:             "sentence_fragment",
   821:             "sentence_fragment_phrase",
   822:             "semantic_anchor_validation_failed",
   823:             "semantic_closure_incomplete",
   824:             "standalone_semantic_integrity_failed",
   825:         }
   826: 
   827:         if not gate_result.get("keep") and hard_pool_reject:
   828:             continue
   829: 
   830:         phrase_text = _light_normalize_phrase(str(gate_result.get("phrase") or phrase_text))
   831:         if not phrase_text:
   832:            continue
   833: 
   834:         if _matches_builder_noise_pattern(phrase_text):
   835:            continue
   836: 
   837:         canonical_text = _light_normalize_phrase(_clean_text(clean_rec.get("canonical") or phrase_text))

FILE: backend\server\stores\upload_document_extractor.py

MATCH LINE: 2
PATTERN: Upload Document
CODE: Universal Upload Document Extractor
------------------------------------------------------------
     1: """
     2: Universal Upload Document Extractor
     3: 
     4: Phase 4.4 responsibility:
     5: - Extract readable body content from uploaded documents.
     6: - Normalize uploaded source content before semantic processing.
     7: - Keep uploaded document extraction separate from website article extraction.
     8: 
     9: Supports: TXT, Markdown, HTML, DOCX.
    10: 
    11: CONTRACT (fixed in this version):
    12: - Extracted `text` preserves PARAGRAPH BOUNDARIES as blank lines ("\n\n").
    13:   Downstream (UDUC `_paragraphs_from_content_body`) splits paragraphs on
    14:   blank lines; the previous normalizer removed every blank line, so every
    15:   document collapsed into a single paragraph downstream.
    16: """
    17: 
    18: from __future__ import annotations
    19: 
    20: import re
    21: from dataclasses import dataclass, asdict
    22: from datetime import datetime, timezone
    23: from pathlib import Path
    24: from typing import Any, Dict, List, Optional
    25: 
    26: # Anchor all filesystem defaults to the package (backend/server), matching
    27: # files.py, instead of relying on the process CWD.
    28: BASE_DIR = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]  # backend/server
    29: DEFAULT_EXTRACTION_ARTIFACT_DIR = BASE_DIR / "data" / "upload_extractions"
    30: 
    31: 
    32: @dataclass
    33: class UploadExtractionResult:
    34:     source_path: str
    35:     source_type: str
    36:     title: str
    37:     text: str
    38:     headings: List[str]
    39:     metadata: Dict[str, Any]
    40:     extraction_status: str
    41:     extraction_confidence: float
    42:     created_at: str
    43: 
    44: 
    45: SUPPORTED_UPLOAD_EXTENSIONS = {
    46:     ".txt": "txt",
    47:     ".md": "markdown",
    48:     ".markdown": "markdown",
    49:     ".html": "html",
    50:     ".htm": "html",
    51:     ".docx": "docx",
    52: }
    53: 
    54: 
    55: def detect_upload_source_type(path: str | Path) -> str:
    56:     p = Path(path)
    57:     return SUPPORTED_UPLOAD_EXTENSIONS.get(p.suffix.lower(), "unsupported")
    58:
